Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..20)
find y: int(0..5)
find z: int(0..6)

such that

(x / (y / z)) = 10

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#int_offset_1: bool
  find x#int_offset_2: bool
  find x#int_offset_3: bool
  find x#int_offset_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#int_offset_4),or([!(x#int_offset_3),or([!(x#int_offset_2),or([!(x#int_offset_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Offset, [x#int_offset_1,x#int_offset_2,x#int_offset_3,x#int_offset_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) >= 5,SATInt(Offset, [x#int_offset_1,x#int_offset_2,x#int_offset_3,x#int_offset_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) <= 20;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
